Boats arriving from Tangyang pay full inward dues if discharging at Shasi town, local trade dues if discharging near the West station; but in the Shipping table they are all entered as local traders. In the same way boats from Shasi town to Tangyang pay full outward dues, but are classed among local clearances. Boats arriving from Tangyang for other ports pay full outward dues. They are entered in the Shipping table first as local entries, then as interpor learances. Boats arriving from other ports for Tangyang have usually no dues to pay, but in the Shipping table they are entered, firstly, as interport entries and, secondly, as local clearances. 272 OUTOMS GAZETTE. [OOT.-DlC&#39;., NOTICES TO MARINERS. CHINA SEA. No. 458. YANGTZE RIVER-KIUKIANG DISTRICT. SHANSI POINT BEACON SHIFTED FURTHER INLAND. NOTICE is hereby given that, owing to the washing away of the river bank, Shansi Point Beacon has been shifted 827 feet S. 89* E. (magnetic) further inland. SHANGHAI, ISt October i9o8. No. 459. SHANGHAI DISTRICT. ENTRANCE TO HANGCHdW BAY. YANGTZE CAPE BELL BUOY. NOTICE is hereby given that the Yangtze Cape Bell Buoy has been removed from its former position and has been placed off the steep-to bank to the southward of Yangtze Cape, to mark the entrance to Hangehow Bay. From the buoy Gutzlaff Lighthouse bears S. 76* E. (magnetic), distant 14.4 miles. The buoy has been changed in colour from red and black vertical stripes to red. SHANGHAI, I6th October 1908.
1908.] NOTIFICATIONS. 273 No. 460. WENCHOW DISTRICT. SHROUD ISLAND LIGHT ESTABLISHED. NOTICE is hereby giv,en that Shroud Island Light was exhibited for the first time at sunset on the ist November 1908. The illuminating apparatus is dioptric, revolving, group-flashing, of the fourth order, showing five white flashes in quick succession every 20 seconds, thus:- Flash . . . . . . . . . . . . seconds. Eclipse..... .........2 Flash..... ............ Eclipse..... .........2 Flash..... ............ Eclipse..... .........2 Flash..... ............ Eclipse. .........2 Flash..... ............ Eclipse . . . . . . . . . 8 ,, and so on. The approximate power of the flash is 14,ooo candles. The lighthouse stands on. the western extremity of Shroud Island, and the light, which is elevated 233 feet above the level of the sea, shows all round, and should be visible in clear weather at a distance of 21 nautical miles. The tower is a steel struoture, with a total height from base to lantern vane of 26 feet. The tower and dwellings are painted white. Approximate position:- Latitude... ..............27&#39; 38&#39; N. Longitude . . . . . . . . . . 1210 3&#39; E. SHANGHAI, 26th December 19o8. 18
